Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a neuro algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 848. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 454/3.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to neuro structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 848/454 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
